# FraudFlow Intelligence - Step-by-Step Hosting Guide (SIH26184)

This guide walks you through hosting your FraudFlow Intelligence platform online with a public HTTPS URL so that evaluators, hackathon judges, or team members can access it from anywhere.

---

## Which Hosting Method Should You Choose?

| Method | Best For | Setup Time | Cost | Link Type |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Option 1: Render.com** *(Recommended)* | Permanent 24/7 cloud hosting | 5 minutes | Free | `https://fraudflow.onrender.com` |
| **Option 2: Cloudflare Tunnel** | Instant live link for hackathon evaluation / demo | 2 minutes | Free | `https://xxxx.trycloudflare.com` |
| **Option 3: Railway.app** | Modern developer cloud platform | 3 minutes | Free trial | `https://fraudflow.up.railway.app` |
| **Option 4: Vercel** | Serverless cloud hosting | 5 minutes | Free | `https://fraudflow.vercel.app` |

---

## Option 1: Deploy on Render.com (Recommended for 24/7 Cloud Hosting)

Render provides a native Python web service that automatically installs your dependencies from `requirements.txt` and runs `uvicorn app.main:app`.

### Step 1: Upload Your Project to GitHub
1. Go to [github.com](https://github.com) and log in (or create a free account).
2. Click the **+** icon in the top right -> **New repository**.
3. Name your repository: `fraudflow-intelligence`.
4. Choose **Public** or **Private** and click **Create repository**.
5. Upload your files:
   - Click **"uploading an existing file"** link on the empty repo page.
   - Drag and drop the contents of your project folder (`app`, `data`, `api`, `requirements.txt`, `Procfile`, `render.yaml`, `README.md`, etc.).
   - Click **Commit changes**.
   *(Alternatively, use GitHub Desktop or Git if installed).*

### Step 2: Create a Web Service on Render
1. Go to [render.com](https://render.com) and sign up/log in using your GitHub account.
2. From the Render Dashboard, click **New +** -> **Web Service**.
3. Select **"Build and deploy from a Git repository"** -> click **Next**.
4. Connect your `fraudflow-intelligence` repository.

### Step 3: Configure Build & Start Settings
Fill in the following fields (Render may auto-detect them from `render.yaml` and `Procfile`):
- **Name**: `fraudflow-intelligence` (or any custom name)
- **Region**: Choose the closest region (e.g., `Singapore` or `Frankfurt`)
- **Branch**: `main`
- **Runtime**: `Python 3`
- **Build Command**:
  ```bash
  pip install -r requirements.txt
  ```
- **Start Command**:
  ```bash
  uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port $PORT
  ```
- **Instance Type**: **Free**

### Step 4: Deploy
1. Click **Deploy Web Service** at the bottom.
2. Render will build the environment, install packages, and launch your server.
3. Within 2–3 minutes, your live URL will appear at the top:
   ```text
   https://fraudflow-intelligence.onrender.com
   ```

---

## Option 2: Instant Public URL via Cloudflare Tunnel (Fastest for Live Demos / Judges)

If you need a public HTTPS link **right now** for a presentation or evaluation without setting up GitHub or cloud accounts, you can expose your locally running server (`http://127.0.0.1:8001`) securely using Cloudflare Tunnel.

### Step 1: Download Cloudflared
1. Open PowerShell on your computer.
2. Download the official standalone `cloudflared.exe` from Cloudflare:
   ```powershell
   winget install --id Cloudflare.cloudflared
   ```
   *Or download `cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe` directly from [github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/releases](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/releases).*

### Step 2: Launch the Tunnel
Ensure your local server is running on port 8001:
```powershell
python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8001
```
In a second terminal window, run:
```powershell
cloudflared tunnel --url http://127.0.0.1:8001
```

### Step 3: Share the URL
Cloudflare will output a public HTTPS address in your terminal:
```text
https://xxxx-xxxx-xxxx.trycloudflare.com
```
Anyone in the world can immediately open this link in any browser on phone or PC to view and test your live platform!

---

## Option 3: Deploy on Railway.app

1. Go to [railway.app](https://railway.app) and sign in with GitHub.
2. Click **New Project** -> **Deploy from GitHub repo**.
3. Select your `fraudflow-intelligence` repository.
4. Click **Deploy Now**.
5. Once deployed:
   - Go to **Settings** -> **Networking** -> click **Generate Domain**.
   - Your public link (e.g., `https://fraudflow-intelligence.up.railway.app`) will be active.

---

## Option 4: Deploy on Vercel

The project already includes `vercel.json` and the serverless entrypoint `api/index.py`.

1. Push your project to GitHub.
2. Visit [vercel.com/new](https://vercel.com/new) and log in with GitHub.
3. Click **Import** next to `fraudflow-intelligence`.
4. Leave Framework Preset as **Other**.
5. Click **Deploy**.
6. Vercel builds the serverless functions and gives you a `https://fraudflow-intelligence.vercel.app` URL.

---

## Pre-Deployment Checklist

Before sharing your public URL with judges or evaluators, verify:
- [x] Web dashboard loads at `/`
- [x] API health check responds at `/api/health` with `{"status": "HEALTHY"}`
- [x] Case analysis works for `CASE-DIN-2026-001`
- [x] Forensic Blockchain Explorer modal verifies with `100% VERIFIED`
- [x] Crypto & VDA Tracing tab traces on-chain hops and generates Section 102 CrPC freeze directives
- [x] Zero console errors or 404s

# Vercel Deployment Guide - FraudFlow Intelligence

This repository is pre-configured with `vercel.json` and a serverless entrypoint `api/index.py` for deployment to **Vercel**.

---

## Quick Method 1: Deploy via Vercel CLI (Fastest)

1. Open your terminal / command prompt in this directory:
   ```bash
   cd fraudflow-intelligence
   ```

2. Install the Vercel CLI globally (if not already installed):
   ```bash
   npm i -g vercel
   ```

3. Log in to your Vercel account:
   ```bash
   vercel login
   ```

4. Deploy directly:
   ```bash
   vercel
   ```
   - When asked `Set up and deploy?`, press `y`.
   - Choose your Vercel scope/account.
   - Link to existing project? `N`.
   - What's your project's name? `fraudflow-intelligence`.
   - In which directory is your code located? `./`.
   - Want to modify these settings? `N`.

5. For production deployment:
   ```bash
   vercel --prod
   ```

Your dashboard will be live at `https://fraudflow-intelligence.vercel.app` (or your chosen URL)!

---

## Quick Method 2: Deploy via GitHub (Recommended for SIH Evaluation Portal)

1. **Initialize Git & Push to GitHub**:
   ```bash
   git init
   git add .
   git commit -m "feat: SIH26184 National Level Predictive Analytics Platform"
   git branch -M main
   git remote add origin https://github.com/YOUR_GITHUB_USERNAME/fraudflow-intelligence.git
   git push -u origin main
   ```

2. **Connect to Vercel**:
   - Go to [vercel.com/new](https://vercel.com/new)
   - Click **Import** next to your `fraudflow-intelligence` repository.
   - Leave the Framework Preset as **Other**.
   - Click **Deploy**.

Vercel will detect `api/index.py` and `vercel.json`, install dependencies from `requirements.txt`, and generate your public HTTPS URL.

---

## Local Development (Port 8001)

To run the platform locally on Windows:
```cmd
run.bat
```
Or run directly with Python:
```bash
python -m uvicorn app.main:app --reload --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8001
```
Open your browser at:
**http://127.0.0.1:8001**
